{\n    // A is stored in row-major order\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j <= i; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            \n            if (i == j) {\n OpenMP tasks with\n// depend clauses express the resulting dependency DAG so that independent tile\n// operations (in particular the O(n^3) trailing-submatrix updates) run concurrently.\n\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 64;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky factorization restricted to the diagonal tile [off, off+bs).\nstatic bool potrfBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t off, const size_t bs) {\n    for (size_t i = off; i < off + bs; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = off; j <= i; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            if (i == j) {\n                for (size_t k = off; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n                    return false;\n                }\n                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = off; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Triangular solve: computes tile L[i][k] = A[i][k] * inv(L[k][k])^T given the\n// already-factorized diagonal tile L[k][k].\nstatic void trsmBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t ig = iOff; ig < iOff + iSize; ++ig) {\n        for (size_t jg = kOff; jg < kOff + kSize; ++jg) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < jg; ++p) {\n                sum += A[ig * n + p] * A[jg * n + p];\n            }\n            A[ig * n + jg] = (A[ig * n + jg] - sum) / A[jg * n + jg];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing submatrix update for a diagonal tile: A[i][i] -= L[i][k] * L[i][k]^T\n// (only the lower triangle is needed/updated).\nstatic void syrkBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t r = iOff; r < iOff + iSize; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = iOff; c <= r; ++c) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < kOff + kSize; ++p) {\n                sum += A[r * n + p] * A[c * n + p];\n            }\n            A[r * n + c] -= sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing submatrix update for an off-diagonal tile: A[i][j] -= L[i][k] * L[j][k]^T\nstatic void gemmBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t jOff, const size_t jSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t r = iOff; r < iOff + iSize; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = jOff; c < jOff + jSize; ++c) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < kOff + kSize; ++p) {\n                sum += A[r * n + p] * A[c * n + p];\n            }\n            A[r * n + c] -= sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const size_t nb = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    // Dummy per-tile variables used purely as addresses for OpenMP task\n    // dependency tracking (depend clauses key on address identity, not value).\n    std::vector<char> tags(nb * nb);\n    std::atomic<bool> posDef{true};\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        #pragma omp single\n        {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n                const size_t kOff = k * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                const size_t kSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - kOff);\n                char* kkTag = &tags[k * nb + k];\n\n                #pragma omp task depend(inout: kkTag[0]) shared(A, posDef)\n                {\n                    if (posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                        if (!potrfBlock(A, n, kOff, kSize)) {\n                            posDef.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                    const size_t iOff = i * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                    const size_t iSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - iOff);\n                    char* ikTag = &tags[i * nb + k];\n\n                    for (size_t j = k + 1; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; OpenMP tasks with\n// depend clauses express the resulting dependency DAG so that independent tile\n// operations (in particular the O(n^3) trailing-submatrix updates) run concurrently.\n\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 64;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky factorization restricted to the diagonal tile [off, off+bs).\nstatic bool potrfBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t off, const size_t bs) {\n    for (size_t i = off; OpenMP tasks with\n// depend clauses express the resulting dependency DAG so that independent tile\n// operations (in particular the O(n^3) trailing-submatrix updates) run concurrently.\n\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 64;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky factorization restricted to the diagonal tile [off, off+bs).\nstatic bool potrfBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t off, const size_t bs) {\n    for (size_t i = off; i < off + bs; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = off; j <= i; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            if (i == j) {\n                for (size_t k = off; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n                    return false;\n                }\n                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = off; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Triangular solve: computes tile L[i][k] = A[i][k] * inv(L[k][k])^T given the\n// already-factorized diagonal tile L[k][k].\nstatic void trsmBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t ig = iOff; ig < iOff + iSize; ++ig) {\n        for (size_t jg = kOff; jg < kOff + kSize; ++jg) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < jg; ++p) {\n                sum += A[ig * n + p] * A[jg * n + p];\n            }\n            A[ig * n + jg] = (A[ig * n + jg] - sum) / A[jg * n + jg];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing submatrix update for a diagonal tile: A[i][i] -= L[i][k] * L[i][k]^T\n// (only the lower triangle is needed/updated).\nstatic void syrkBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t r = iOff; r < iOff + iSize; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = iOff; c <= r; ++c) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < kOff + kSize; ++p) {\n                sum += A[r * n + p] * A[c * n + p];\n            }\n            A[r * n + c] -= sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing submatrix update for an off-diagonal tile: A[i][j] -= L[i][k] * L[j][k]^T\nstatic void gemmBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t jOff, const size_t jSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t r = iOff; r < iOff + iSize; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = jOff; c < jOff + jSize; ++c) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < kOff + kSize; ++p) {\n                sum += A[r * n + p] * A[c * n + p];\n            }\n            A[r * n + c] -= sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const size_t nb = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    // Dummy per-tile variables used purely as addresses for OpenMP task\n    // dependency tracking (depend clauses key on address identity, not value).\n    std::vector<char> tags(nb * nb);\n    std::atomic<bool> posDef{true};\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        #pragma omp single\n        {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n                const size_t kOff = k * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                const size_t kSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - kOff);\n                #pragma omp task depend(inout: tags[k * nb + k]) shared(A, posDef)\n                {\n                    if (posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                        if (!potrfBlock(A, n, kOff, kSize)) {\n                            posDef.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                    const size_t iOff = i * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                    const size_t iSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - iOff);\n                    #pragma omp task depend(in: tags[k * nb + k]) depend(inout: tags[i * nb + k]) shared(A, posDef)\n                    {\n                        if (posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                            trsmBlock(A, n, iOff, iSize, kOff, kSize);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                    const size_t iOff = i * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                    const size_t iSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - iOff);\n                    char* ikTag = &tags[i * nb + k];\n\n                    for (size_t j = k + 1; j <= i; ++j) {\n                        const size_t jOff = j * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                        const size_t jSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - jOff);\n                        char* jkTag = &tags[j * nb + k];\n                        char* ijTag = &tags[i * nb + j];\n\n                        if (i == j) {\n                            #pragma omp task depend(in: ikTag[0]) depend(inout: ijTag[0]) shared(A, posDef)\n                            {\n                                if (posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                                    syrkBlock(A, n, iOff, iSize, kOff, kSize);\n                                }\n                            }\n                        } else {\n                            #pragma omp task depend(in: ikTag[0], jkTag[0]) depend(inout: ijTag[0]) shared(A, posDef)\n                            {\n                                if (posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                                    gemmBlock(A, n, iOff, iSize, jOff, jSize, kOff, kSize);\n                                }\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (!posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    // Zero out the upper triangular part\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; OpenMP tasks with\n// depend clauses express the resulting dependency DAG so that independent tile\n// operations (in particular the O(n^3) trailing-submatrix updates) run concurrently.\n\nconstexpr size_t BLOCK_SIZE = 64;\n\n// Unblocked Cholesky factorization restricted to the diagonal tile [off, off+bs).\nstatic bool potrfBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t off, const size_t bs) {\n    for (size_t i = off; i < off + bs; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = off; j <= i; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            if (i == j) {\n                for (size_t k = off; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    printf(\"Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\\n\", j);\n                    return false;\n                }\n                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n            } else {\n                for (size_t k = off; k < j; ++k) {\n                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];\n                }\n                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Triangular solve: computes tile L[i][k] = A[i][k] * inv(L[k][k])^T given the\n// already-factorized diagonal tile L[k][k].\nstatic void trsmBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t ig = iOff; ig < iOff + iSize; ++ig) {\n        for (size_t jg = kOff; jg < kOff + kSize; ++jg) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < jg; ++p) {\n                sum += A[ig * n + p] * A[jg * n + p];\n            }\n            A[ig * n + jg] = (A[ig * n + jg] - sum) / A[jg * n + jg];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing submatrix update for a diagonal tile: A[i][i] -= L[i][k] * L[i][k]^T\n// (only the lower triangle is needed/updated).\nstatic void syrkBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t r = iOff; r < iOff + iSize; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = iOff; c <= r; ++c) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < kOff + kSize; ++p) {\n                sum += A[r * n + p] * A[c * n + p];\n            }\n            A[r * n + c] -= sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Trailing submatrix update for an off-diagonal tile: A[i][j] -= L[i][k] * L[j][k]^T\nstatic void gemmBlock(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                       const size_t iOff, const size_t iSize,\n                       const size_t jOff, const size_t jSize,\n                       const size_t kOff, const size_t kSize) {\n    for (size_t r = iOff; r < iOff + iSize; ++r) {\n        for (size_t c = jOff; c < jOff + jSize; ++c) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t p = kOff; p < kOff + kSize; ++p) {\n                sum += A[r * n + p] * A[c * n + p];\n            }\n            A[r * n + c] -= sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    const size_t nb = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    // Dummy per-tile variables used purely as addresses for OpenMP task\n    // dependency tracking (depend clauses key on address identity, not value).\n    std::vector<char> tags(nb * nb);\n    char* tagsPtr = tags.data();\n    std::atomic<bool> posDef{true};\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        #pragma omp single\n        {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < nb; ++k) {\n                const size_t kOff = k * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                const size_t kSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - kOff);\n                #pragma omp task depend(inout: tagsPtr[k * nb + k]) shared(A, posDef)\n                {\n                    if (posDef.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                        if (!potrfBlock(A, n, kOff, kSize)) {\n                            posDef.store(false, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n\n                for (size_t i = k + 1; i < nb; ++i) {\n                    const size_t iOff = i * BLOCK_SIZE;\n                    const size_t iSize = std::min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - iOff);\n\n                    for (size_t j = k + 1; j <= i; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {\n    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random\n    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite\n    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite\n    \n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n    \n    // Generate random matrix B\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n    \n    // Compute A = B * B^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];\n            }\n            A[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i * n + i] += n;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n    \n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n    \n    // Compute L * L^T\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max: maxError, relError)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n    \n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Allocate matrix\n    std::vector<double> A(n * n);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n    \n    // Generate positive definite matrix\n    printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);\n    \n    if (validate) {\n        A_orig = A;
